Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The electrical variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 754 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 436 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by electrical generators. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 436. QED.
